HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001143/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_024a52fb10f56b576378","text":"3. **A crowd converted into a laid-out field.** Alongside the insect branch, `ف ر ش B001` supplies spreading, leveling, and laying out, reinforced by `ب ث ث B001`. The following parallel transformation of mountains into loosened fibers in 101:5 supplies a material analogy: compact aggregates become distributed matter. The branch role is the resulting spatial state, while the moth branch supplies the motion that produces it. I infer the motion-to-surface arrow; the ayah itself only joins simile and participle. This activation lets `كَٱلْفَرَاشِ` describe both fluttering agents and a formerly organized population laid out without collective structure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001332/B001","root_001700/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0a39a65df21af22e68dd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:4:1\",\"root_001700\",[\"B003\"]],[\"101:4:2\",\"root_001332\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Gün, bu dönüşümlerin yalnız gerçekleştiği bir zaman değil, insanların ve dağların değişmesiyle bizzat kurulan şiddetli olaydır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001700/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1f13d0ffda440cd43185","text":"1. **An event-time constituted by transformation.** `ي و م B003` supplies a severe occasion or event-day, and `ك و ن B001` supplies occurrence or temporal predication. The line follows the threefold `ٱلْقَارِعَةُ` sequence and is coordinated with the second `تَكُونُ` in 101:5. `يَوْمَ` therefore marks not ordinary clock time but the occasion whose content is the paired becoming of people and mountains. I infer that the transformations constitute the event rather than merely happen during it. The ayah reads as the first internal mechanism of the named occurrence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000083/B001","root_001143/B001","root_001143/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_370251858baad3839486","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:4:4\",\"root_001143\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"101:4:5\",\"root_000083\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Saçılan insanlar, açık bir yüzeye serilmiş ve toplu düzenini kaybetmiş madde gibi de görünür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000083/B002","root_001143/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_38a6dac060fd43161857","text":"101:11 made the moth branch of ف ر ش B005 more dangerous: the swarm is implicitly oriented toward fire. 101:6-8 also turned ب ث ث B002 into moral disclosure; the visible scattering anticipates the later weighing of what had been hidden.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000053/B002","root_000083/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_95967a44c23d36a3754e","text":"The coordinated mountain change in 101:5 confirmed that this is a general loss of aggregate cohesion, not only erratic human motion. The scale clauses in 101:6 and 101:8 then activated `ب ث ث B002`: scattering exposes differences hidden by the collective `ٱلنَّاسُ`. Most surprisingly, `أُمُّهُ` in 101:9 supplies a gathering-center branch (`ء م م B002`), correcting any assumption that dispersal is final; it is followed by individual re-centering.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000083/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ae0acb399e6a60c16081","text":"4. **External scattering as disclosure of hidden difference.** `ب ث ث B002` covers disclosing what is hidden in the self. After the universal `ٱلنَّاسُ` is made `مَبْثُوث`, 101:6-9 separates persons by heavy or light balances and distinct outcomes. The disclosure branch assigns dispersal an epistemic role: collective appearance is broken open so individually assessed difference can become visible. The packet does not say scattering causes judgment; that revealing arrow is inferred from the move from mass noun to `مَن`. The participle consequently reads as exposure as well as physical distribution.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000083/B001","root_000083/B002","root_000473/B001","root_001143/B001","root_001143/B004","root_001143/B005","root_001219/B010","root_001332/B001","root_001534/B003","root_001700/B003","root_004965/B001","root_004965/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_dbc1ca6dce432f4e1213","text":"1. Event-day as dispersed movement. Evidence: ي و م B003, severe event-day; ك و ن B001, occurrence in time; ن و س B001, swaying/dangling oscillation; ف ر ش B005, fluttering moth-like insect; ب ث ث B001, scattering. Structural cue: the first explanatory image after the triple قارعة is kinetic and plural. Mechanism: the strike converts \"people\" into unstable suspended motion and then into broadcast scattering. Abductive move: I infer that the event-day causes the bodily-social form of الناس to lose coordinated direction. Reading change: the ayah reads less as a static simile and more as a physical mechanism of disorientation. 2. Exposure on a laid-out surface. Evidence: ف ر ش B001/B002, spreading, bedding, prepared surface; ق ر ع B010 earlier, open road/courtyard; ب ث ث B001, laying/scattering out. Structural cue: قارعة as exposed place is followed by فرش/مبثوث. Mechanism: the people are not merely flying; they are displayed across the open plane like matter laid out after impact. Abductive move: I supply the spatial arrow from the named event-place to the human distribution on it. Reading change: الناس become evidence spread across a surface, not simply a frightened crowd. 3. Hidden inner distress made visible. Evidence: ب ث ث B002, disclosing what is hidden in the self; د ر ي B001 in 101:3, making known; و ز ن B002 later, accounting of deeds. Structural cue: the sequence moves from unknowability to visible human dispersal and then to balances. Mechanism: scattering also discloses what had been interior or unreckoned. Abductive move: I infer that external dispersion reveals inner/accountable state. Reading change: المبثوث can carry disclosure, so the ayah reads as the surfacing of concealed moral condition. 4. Herd without handler. Evidence: ن و س B002, driving camels; ن ف ش B003 later, night-roaming livestock without shepherd; ف ر ش B004, small/low livestock. Structural cue: human plurality is analogized with light creatures, then mountains with animal/textile wool. Mechanism: society loses guidance and becomes animal-like motion without a driver. Abductive move: I supply the absence of a controlling shepherd/driver from the uncontrolled spread. Reading change: الناس are read socially as a herd whose normal direction has collapsed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000083/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e3f5f7755aa54441b094","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:4:5\",\"root_000083\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Saçılmanın gizliyi açığa çıkarma boyutu, kalabalığın altında saklı bireysel durumların sonraki tartılarda görünür olmasını sağlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001143/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f588f0703c688754b7c3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:4:4\",\"root_001143\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Dağınık pervane imgesi, son ayetteki ateşle geriye dönük olarak ölümcül bir yönelim kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000083/B001","root_001143/B005","root_004965/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f843a6f12ba8c4d3026e","text":"2. **Loss of stable orientation before dispersal.** The packet roots `ٱلنَّاسُ` in `ن و س B001`, swaying or dangling oscillation; `ف ر ش B005` supplies the light, fluttering moth-like insect; and `ب ث ث B001` supplies visible scattering. Their sequence gives three stages: an unstable collective, fluttering motion, then spatial dispersion. The text does not state a temporal chain among these simultaneous descriptions, so ordering them as a mechanism is abductive. The people are thereby read not just as numerous insects but as bodies deprived of a stable axis and then spread apart.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
